Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the geometric space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 318 and the convergence rate is O(193/n^4).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 318 + O(1/n^193). Summing over all modes and applying the geometric convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the geometric space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 802 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 459. QED.
